# 第四关：04-paste-ident
通过函数式过程宏动态生成rust函数。这里大家可以在主目录通过cargo expand命令，查看生成后的效果。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use proc_macro2;
use syn;

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as SeqParser);
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for i in st.start..st.end {
        ret.extend(st.expand(&st.body, i));
    }
    return ret.into();
}

struct SeqParser {//自定义语法树节点
    variable_ident: syn::Ident,
    start: isize,
    end: isize,
    body: proc_macro2::TokenStream,
}

impl syn::parse::Parse for SeqParser {
    fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self>{
        let variable_ident: syn::Ident = input.parse()?;        //解析当前游标对应的变量
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(in)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token in
        let start: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(..)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token ..
        let end: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                  //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        
        let body_buf;
        syn::braced!(body_buf in input);                        //通过宏braced!把当前游标之后的整个代码块读取过来。
        let body: proc_macro2::TokenStream = body_buf.parse()?;

        Ok(SeqParser{
            variable_ident,
            start: start.base10_parse()?,
            end: end.base10_parse()?,
            body,
        })
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut idx = 0;
        while idx < buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(prefix) => {
                    if idx + 2 < buf.len() {
                        if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) = &buf[idx + 1] {
                            if p.as_char() == '~' {
                                if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) = &buf[idx + 2] {
                                    /*
                                    这里通过三级匹配已经把 f~N 这三个匹配出来了。 prefix:f  ~:p  i:N
                                    然后判断i时候等于用户传入的函数式过程宏的第一个标识符？
                                    判断f 与 ~ 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的?
                                    判断~ 与 i 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的？
                                     */
                                    if i == &self.variable_ident                    
                                        && prefix.span().end() == p.span().start()
                                        && p.span().end() == i.span().start() 
                                    {
                                        let new_ident_litral = format!("{}{}", prefix.to_string(), n);
                                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Ident::new(new_ident_litral.as_str(), prefix.span());
                                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                                        idx += 3;
                                        continue;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if prefix == &self.variable_ident {
                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Literal::i64_unsuffixed(n as i64);
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                        idx += 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
                _ => {
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
            }
            idx += 1;
        }
        ret
    }
}
```
# 第五关：05-repeat-section
这小关考的是，在函数式过程宏内部使用自定义的开头和结尾，处理函数式过程宏的生成规则。
题目当前是函数式过程宏内部通过自定义的#,*开头结尾形成一个循环。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use proc_macro2;
use syn;

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as SeqParser);
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    let buffer = syn::buffer::TokenBuffer::new2(st.body.clone());

    let (ret_1, expanded) = st.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(buffer.begin());
    if expanded {
        return ret_1.into();
    }

    for i in st.start..st.end {
        ret.extend(st.expand(&st.body, i));
    }
    return ret.into();
}

struct SeqParser {//自定义语法树节点
    variable_ident: syn::Ident,
    start: isize,
    end: isize,
    body: proc_macro2::TokenStream,
}

impl syn::parse::Parse for SeqParser {
    fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self>{
        let variable_ident: syn::Ident = input.parse()?;        //解析当前游标对应的变量
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(in)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token in
        let start: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(..)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token ..
        let end: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                  //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        
        let body_buf;
        syn::braced!(body_buf in input);                        //通过宏braced!把当前游标之后的整个代码块读取过来。
        let body: proc_macro2::TokenStream = body_buf.parse()?;

        Ok(SeqParser{
            variable_ident,
            start: start.base10_parse()?,
            end: end.base10_parse()?,
            body,
        })
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut idx = 0;
        while idx < buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(prefix) => {
                    if idx + 2 < buf.len() {
                        if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) = &buf[idx + 1] {
                            if p.as_char() == '~' {
                                if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) = &buf[idx + 2] {
                                    /*
                                    这里通过三级匹配已经把 f~N 这三个匹配出来了。 prefix:f  ~:p  i:N
                                    然后判断i时候等于用户传入的函数式过程宏的第一个标识符？
                                    判断f 与 ~ 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的?
                                    判断~ 与 i 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的？
                                     */
                                    if i == &self.variable_ident                    
                                        && prefix.span().end() == p.span().start()
                                        && p.span().end() == i.span().start() 
                                    {
                                        let new_ident_litral = format!("{}{}", prefix.to_string(), n);
                                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Ident::new(new_ident_litral.as_str(), prefix.span());
                                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                                        idx += 3;
                                        continue;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if prefix == &self.variable_ident {
                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Literal::i64_unsuffixed(n as i64);
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                        idx += 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
                _ => {
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
            }
            idx += 1;
        }
        ret
    }
    fn find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(&self, c: syn::buffer::Cursor) -> (proc_macro2::TokenStream, bool) {
        let mut found = false;
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut cursor = c;
        while !cursor.eof() {
            if let Some((punct_prefix, cursor_1)) = cursor.punct() {
                if punct_prefix.as_char() == '#' {
                    if let Some((group_cur, _, cursor_2)) = cursor_1.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                        if let Some((punct_suffix, cursor_3)) = cursor_2.punct() {
                            if punct_suffix.as_char() == '*' {
                                for i in self.start..self.end {
                                    let t = self.expand(&group_cur.token_stream(), i);
                                    ret.extend(t);
                                }
                                cursor = cursor_3;
                                found = true;
                                continue;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }


            if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Brace) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!({#t}));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Bracket) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!([#t]));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!((#t)));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((punct, next_cur)) = cursor.punct() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#punct));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((ident, next_cur)) = cursor.ident() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#ident));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((literal, next_cur)) = cursor.literal() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#literal));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((lifetime, next_cur)) = cursor.lifetime() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#lifetime));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            }
        }

        (ret, found)
    }
}
```
# 第六关：06-init-array
由于我们之前的代码就可以成功生成当前过程宏的代码，所以在这里不再多做功了。
# 第七关：07-inclusive-range
这一小关实际上只是比之前的多了一个有可能会有“=”的处理。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use proc_macro2;
use syn::{self, Result};

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as SeqParser);
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    let buffer = syn::buffer::TokenBuffer::new2(st.body.clone());

    let (ret_1, expanded) = st.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(buffer.begin());
    if expanded {
        return ret_1.into();
    }

    for i in st.start..st.end {
        ret.extend(st.expand(&st.body, i));
    }
    return ret.into();
}

struct SeqParser {//自定义语法树节点
    variable_ident: syn::Ident,
    start: isize,
    end: isize,
    body: proc_macro2::TokenStream,
}

impl syn::parse::Parse for SeqParser {
    fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self>{
        let variable_ident: syn::Ident = input.parse()?;        //解析当前游标对应的变量
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(in)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token in
        let start: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(..)>()?;                      //解析当前游标对应的token ..
        let mut inc = false;
        if input.peek(syn::Token!(=)) {                         //通过peek判断当前游标对应的token时候是 = ？
            input.parse::<syn::Token!(=)>()?;
            inc = true;
        }
        let end: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                  //假定当前游标对应的是一个可以解析为整形数  字面量的token
        
        let body_buf;
        syn::braced!(body_buf in input);                        //通过宏braced!把当前游标之后的整个代码块读取过来。
        let body: proc_macro2::TokenStream = body_buf.parse()?;

        let mut t = SeqParser{
            variable_ident,
            start: start.base10_parse()?,
            end: end.base10_parse()?,
            body,
        };

        if inc {
            t.end += 1;
        }

        Ok(t)
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut idx = 0;
        while idx < buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(prefix) => {
                    if idx + 2 < buf.len() {
                        if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) = &buf[idx + 1] {
                            if p.as_char() == '~' {
                                if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) = &buf[idx + 2] {
                                    /*
                                    这里通过三级匹配已经把 f~N 这三个匹配出来了。 prefix:f  ~:p  i:N
                                    然后判断i时候等于用户传入的函数式过程宏的第一个标识符？
                                    判断f 与 ~ 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的?
                                    判断~ 与 i 两个span彼此是不是紧密相连的？
                                     */
                                    if i == &self.variable_ident                    
                                        && prefix.span().end() == p.span().start()
                                        && p.span().end() == i.span().start() 
                                    {
                                        let new_ident_litral = format!("{}{}", prefix.to_string(), n);
                                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Ident::new(new_ident_litral.as_str(), prefix.span());
                                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                                        idx += 3;
                                        continue;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if prefix == &self.variable_ident {
                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Literal::i64_unsuffixed(n as i64);
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                        idx += 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
                _ => {
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
            }
            idx += 1;
        }
        ret
    }
    fn find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(&self, c: syn::buffer::Cursor) -> (proc_macro2::TokenStream, bool) {
        let mut found = false;
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut cursor = c;
        while !cursor.eof() {
            if let Some((punct_prefix, cursor_1)) = cursor.punct() {
                if punct_prefix.as_char() == '#' {
                    if let Some((group_cur, _, cursor_2)) = cursor_1.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                        if let Some((punct_suffix, cursor_3)) = cursor_2.punct() {
                            if punct_suffix.as_char() == '*' {
                                for i in self.start..self.end {
                                    let t = self.expand(&group_cur.token_stream(), i);
                                    ret.extend(t);
                                }
                                cursor = cursor_3;
                                found = true;
                                continue;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }


            if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Brace) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!({#t}));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Bracket) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!([#t]));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!((#t)));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((punct, next_cur)) = cursor.punct() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#punct));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((ident, next_cur)) = cursor.ident() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#ident));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((literal, next_cur)) = cursor.literal() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#literal));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((lifetime, next_cur)) = cursor.lifetime() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#lifetime));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            }
        }

        (ret, found)
    }
}
```
# 第八关：08-ident-span
早在我们做第四关的时候，已经获取了第一个标识符的span作为新ident的span。所以错误信息是不会错的。所以可以直接通过。

use syn;

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as SeqParser);
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for i in st.start..st.end {

}

struct SeqParser {//\u81EA\u5B9A\u4E49\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9
    variable_ident: syn::Ident,
    start: isize,
    end: isize,

impl syn::parse::Parse for SeqParser {
    fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self>{
        let variable_ident: syn::Ident = input.parse()?;        //\u89E3\u6790\u5F53\u524D\u6E38\u6807\u5BF9\u5E94\u7684\u53D8\u91CF

        let end: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                  //\u5047\u5B9A\u5F53\u524D\u6E38\u6807\u5BF9\u5E94\u7684\u662F\u4E00\u4E2A\u53EF\u4EE5\u89E3\u6790\u4E3A\u6574\u5F62\u6570  \u5B57\u9762\u91CF\u7684token

        let body: proc_macro2::TokenStream = body_buf.parse()?;

        Ok(SeqParser{
            variable_ident,
            start: start.base10_parse()?,
            end: end.base10_parse()?,
            body,
        })
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        for idx in 0..buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) => {
                    if i == &self.variable_ident {
                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Literal::i64_unsuffixed(n as i64);
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                    } else {
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                    }
                }
                _ => {
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
            }
        }
        ret
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut idx = 0;
        while idx < buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(prefix) => {
                    if idx + 2 < buf.len() {
                        if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) = &buf[idx + 1] {
                            if p.as_char() == '~' {
                                if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) = &buf[idx + 2] {
                                    /*

use proc_macro2;
use syn;

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as SeqParser);
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    let buffer = syn::buffer::TokenBuffer::new2(st.body.clone());

    let (ret_1, expanded) = st.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(buffer.begin());
    if expanded {
        return ret_1.into();
    }

    for i in st.start..st.end {
        ret.extend(st.expand(&st.body, i));
    }
    return ret.into();
}

struct SeqParser {//\u81EA\u5B9A\u4E49\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9
    variable_ident: syn::Ident,
    start: isize,
    end: isize,
    body: proc_macro2::TokenStream,
}

impl syn::parse::Parse for SeqParser {
    fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self>{
        let variable_ident: syn::Ident = input.parse()?;        //\u89E3\u6790\u5F53\u524D\u6E38\u6807\u5BF9\u5E94\u7684\u53D8\u91CF
        input.parse::<syn::Token!(in)>()?;                      //\u89E3\u6790\u5F53\u524D\u6E38\u6807\u5BF9\u5E94\u7684token in

        let end: syn::LitInt = input.parse()?;                  //\u5047\u5B9A\u5F53\u524D\u6E38\u6807\u5BF9\u5E94\u7684\u662F\u4E00\u4E2A\u53EF\u4EE5\u89E3\u6790\u4E3A\u6574\u5F62\u6570  \u5B57\u9762\u91CF\u7684token

            variable_ident,
            start: start.base10_parse()?,
            end: end.base10_parse()?,
            body,
        })
    }
}

impl SeqParser {
    fn expand(&self, ts: &proc_macro2::TokenStream, n: isize) -> proc_macro2::TokenStream {
        let buf = ts.clone().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut idx = 0;
        while idx < buf.len() {
            let tree_node = &buf[idx];
            match tree_node {
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Group(g) => {
                    let new_stream = self.expand(&g.stream(), n);
                    let wrap_in_group = proc_macro2::Group::new(g.delimiter(), new_stream);
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#wrap_in_group));
                }
                proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(prefix) => {
                    if idx + 2 < buf.len() {
                        if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Punct(p) = &buf[idx + 1] {
                            if p.as_char() == '~' {
                                if let proc_macro2::TokenTree::Ident(i) = &buf[idx + 2] {
                                    /*
                                    \u8FD9\u91CC\u901A\u8FC7\u4E09\u7EA7\u5339\u914D\u5DF2\u7ECF\u628A f~N \u8FD9\u4E09\u4E2A\u5339\u914D\u51FA\u6765\u4E86\u3002 prefix:f  ~:p  i:N
                                    \u7136\u540E\u5224\u65ADi\u65F6\u5019\u7B49\u4E8E\u7528\u6237\u4F20\u5165\u7684\u51FD\u6570\u5F0F\u8FC7\u7A0B\u5B8F\u7684\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u6807\u8BC6\u7B26\uFF1F
                                    \u5224\u65ADf \u4E0E ~ \u4E24\u4E2Aspan\u5F7C\u6B64\u662F\u4E0D\u662F\u7D27\u5BC6\u76F8\u8FDE\u7684?
                                    \u5224\u65AD~ \u4E0E i \u4E24\u4E2Aspan\u5F7C\u6B64\u662F\u4E0D\u662F\u7D27\u5BC6\u76F8\u8FDE\u7684\uFF1F
                                     */
                                    if i == &self.variable_ident                    
                                        && prefix.span().end() == p.span().start()
                                        && p.span().end() == i.span().start() 
                                    {
                                        let new_ident_litral = format!("{}{}", prefix.to_string(), n);
                                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Ident::new(new_ident_litral.as_str(), prefix.span());
                                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                                        idx += 3;
                                        continue;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if prefix == &self.variable_ident {
                        let new_ident = proc_macro2::Literal::i64_unsuffixed(n as i64);
                        ret.extend(quote::quote!(#new_ident));
                        idx += 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
                _ => {
                    ret.extend(quote::quote!(#tree_node));
                }
            }
            idx += 1;
        }
        ret
    }
    fn find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(&self, c: syn::buffer::Cursor) -> (proc_macro2::TokenStream, bool) {
        let mut found = false;
        let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

        let mut cursor = c;
        while !cursor.eof() {
            if let Some((punct_prefix, cursor_1)) = cursor.punct() {
                if punct_prefix.as_char() == '#' {
                    if let Some((group_cur, _, cursor_2)) = cursor_1.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                        if let Some((punct_suffix, cursor_3)) = cursor_2.punct() {
                            if punct_suffix.as_char() == '*' {
                                for i in self.start..self.end {
                                    let t = self.expand(&group_cur.token_stream(), i);
                                    ret.extend(t);
                                }
                                cursor = cursor_3;
                                found = true;
                                continue;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }


            if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Brace) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!({#t}));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Bracket) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!([#t]));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((group_cur, _, next_cur)) = cursor.group(proc_macro2::Delimiter::Parenthesis) {
                let (t, f) = self.find_block_to_expand_and_do_expand(group_cur);
                found = f;
                ret.extend(quote::quote!((#t)));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((punct, next_cur)) = cursor.punct() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#punct));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((ident, next_cur)) = cursor.ident() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#ident));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((literal, next_cur)) = cursor.literal() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#literal));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            } else if let Some((lifetime, next_cur)) = cursor.lifetime() {
                ret.extend(quote::quote!(#lifetime));
                cursor = next_cur;
                continue;
            }
        }

        (ret, found)
    }
}

#[proc_macro]
pub fn seq(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {

| 接下来我们来实现第大关卡debug过程宏的实现。这里主要考的也是派生宏的实现。 |              |

# 第一关：01-parse.rs。
这一关直接返回空TokenStream就可以了。说白了就是我们把这个派生宏的过程宏的一个空架子搭起来就行了。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug)]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

    return Ok(ret);
}

```
# 第二关：02-impl-debug.rs
这一关主要要求实现类似Debug一样过程宏的，可以打印结构体的一个过程宏的功能。其原理就是为结构体实现一个实现std::fmt::Debug的行为，剩下的就是字符串拼接的事情了。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug)]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut fields_stream = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        fields_stream.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &self.#field_name_ident)
        ));
    }

    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident {
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}
```
# 第三关：03-custom-format.rs
增加了过程宏中派生宏里边惰性属性的功能。为特定字段增加打印限制。第三关实际上是利用format_args!这个宏解决了特定格式的格式化打印功能。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug, attributes(debug))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut fields_stream = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();

    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        fields_stream.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
        
    }

    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident {
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

```
# 第四关：04-type-parameter.rs
考的是结构体中包含泛型参数的时候，在impl中如何处理。
这里主要的问题是结构体增加了泛型参数，导致我们生成的impl没有增加泛型参数而报错，无法通过测试。这块可以通过split_for_impl这个函数来获得我们想要的信息。[https://docs.rs/syn/1.0/syn/struct.Generics.html#method.split_for_impl](https://docs.rs/syn/1.0/syn/struct.Generics.html#method.split_for_impl)
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned, parse_quote};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug, attributes(debug))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//把Debug的这个tait增加到原有的语法树节点上
        }
    }

    // 通过split_for_impl()函数把房型抽取出来形成三个impl的片段
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

```
# 第五关：05-phantom-data.rs
本小关卡，考的是结构体内部包含的结构体，已经实现了Debug后，如何处理这种问题。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned, parse_quote};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug, attributes(debug))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_member_generic_type_name(field: &syn::Field, member_name: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ref arguments
        }) = segments.last() {
            if ident == member_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{
                    args,
                    ..
                }) = arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Path(ref gp))) = args.first() {
                        if let Some(generic_ident) = gp.path.segments.first() {
                            return Ok(Some(generic_ident.ident.to_string()));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn get_field_type_name(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ..
        }) = segments.last() {
            return Ok(Some(ident.to_string()));
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
    let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
    for field in fields {
        if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
            field_type_names.push(s);
        }
        if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
            type_param_names.push(s);
        }
    }

    eprintln!("field_type_name:{:?}", field_type_names);
    eprintln!("type_param_names:{:?}", type_param_names);

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
            if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                eprintln!(">>>>>>continue>>>>>>{} {:#?}", type_param_name, g);
                continue;
            }
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//增加trait限定；(把Debug的这个tait增加到原有的语法树节点上)
        }
    }

    // 通过split_for_impl()函数把房型抽取出来形成三个impl的片段
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

```
# 第七关：07-associated-types.rs
这关考的是，泛型的关联类型为debug的情况下如何解决问题。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned, parse_quote};
use syn::visit::{self, Visit};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};
use std::collections::HashMap;

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug, attributes(debug))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_member_generic_type_name(field: &syn::Field, member_name: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ref arguments
        }) = segments.last() {
            if ident == member_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{
                    args,
                    ..
                }) = arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Path(ref gp))) = args.first() {
                        if let Some(generic_ident) = gp.path.segments.first() {
                            return Ok(Some(generic_ident.ident.to_string()));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn get_field_type_name(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ..
        }) = segments.last() {
            return Ok(Some(ident.to_string()));
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

struct TypePathVisitor {
    generic_type_names: Vec<String>,
    associated_types: HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>>,
}

impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for TypePathVisitor {
    fn visit_type_path(&mut self, node:&'ast syn::TypePath) {
        eprintln!("node:{:#?}", node.path.segments);
        if node.path.segments.len() >= 2 {
            eprintln!("len > 2: {:#?}", node.path.segments);
            let generic_type_name = node.path.segments[0].ident.to_string();
            if self.generic_type_names.contains(&generic_type_name) {
                self.associated_types.entry(generic_type_name).or_insert(Vec::new()).push(node.clone());
            }
        }
        visit::visit_type_path(self, node);
    }
}
fn get_generic_associated_types(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>> {
    let origin_generic_param_names: Vec<String> = st.generics.params.iter().filter_map(
        |f| {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(ty) = f {
                return Some(ty.ident.to_string());
            }
            None
        }
    ).collect();

    let mut visitor = TypePathVisitor {
        generic_type_names: origin_generic_param_names,
        associated_types: HashMap::new(),
    };

    visitor.visit_derive_input(st);
    return visitor.associated_types;
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
    let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
    for field in fields {
        if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
            field_type_names.push(s);
        }
        if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
            type_param_names.push(s);
        }
    }

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();//获得DeriveInput中的泛型参数结构体
    let associated_types_map = get_generic_associated_types(st);
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
            if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                continue;
            }
            if associated_types_map.contains_key(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                continue;
            }
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//增加trait限定；(把Debug的这个tait增加到原有的语法树节点上)
        }
    }

    generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
    for (str, associated_types) in associated_types_map {
        eprintln!(">>>associated_types:{}", str);
        for associated_type in associated_types {
            generics_param_to_modify.where_clause.as_mut().unwrap()
                .predicates.push(parse_quote!(#associated_type:std::fmt::Debug));
        }
    }

    // 通过split_for_impl()函数把房型抽取出来形成三个impl的片段
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}
```
# 第八关：08-escape-hatch.rs
这关通过惰性属性手动限定类型。说白了就是如果当我们无法自动匹配限定类型的时候，可以通过派生宏的惰性属性，让用户手动输入限定类型。
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned, parse_quote};
use syn::visit::{self, Visit};
use quote::{ToTokens, quote};
use std::collections::HashMap;

#[proc_macro_derive(CustomDebug, attributes(debug))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructFields = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(d: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructFields> {
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct {
        fields: syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named,
            ..
        }),
        ..
    }) = d.data {
        return Ok(named);
    }
    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(d, "Must define on a Struct, not Enum".to_string()));
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //拼接所有成员变量
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_member_generic_type_name(field: &syn::Field, member_name: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ref arguments
        }) = segments.last() {
            if ident == member_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{
                    args,
                    ..
                }) = arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Path(ref gp))) = args.first() {
                        if let Some(generic_ident) = gp.path.segments.first() {
                            return Ok(Some(generic_ident.ident.to_string()));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn get_field_type_name(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ..
        }) = segments.last() {
            return Ok(Some(ident.to_string()));
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

struct TypePathVisitor {
    generic_type_names: Vec<String>,
    associated_types: HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>>,
}

impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for TypePathVisitor {
    fn visit_type_path(&mut self, node:&'ast syn::TypePath) {
        eprintln!("node:{:#?}", node.path.segments);
        if node.path.segments.len() >= 2 {
            eprintln!("len > 2: {:#?}", node.path.segments);
            let generic_type_name = node.path.segments[0].ident.to_string();
            if self.generic_type_names.contains(&generic_type_name) {
                self.associated_types.entry(generic_type_name).or_insert(Vec::new()).push(node.clone());
            }
        }
        visit::visit_type_path(self, node);
    }
}
fn get_generic_associated_types(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>> {
    let origin_generic_param_names: Vec<String> = st.generics.params.iter().filter_map(
        |f| {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(ty) = f {
                return Some(ty.ident.to_string());
            }
            None
        }
    ).collect();

    let mut visitor = TypePathVisitor {
        generic_type_names: origin_generic_param_names,
        associated_types: HashMap::new(),
    };

    visitor.visit_derive_input(st);
    return visitor.associated_types;
}

fn get_struct_escape_hatch(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> Option<String> {
    if let Some(insert_attr) = st.attrs.last() {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList{
            nested,
            ..
        })) = insert_attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Meta(syn::Meta::NameValue(path_value))) = nested.last() {
                if path_value.path.is_ident("bound") {
                    if let syn::Lit::Str(ref lit) = path_value.lit {
                        return Some(lit.value());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();
    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;
    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();//获得DeriveInput中的泛型参数结构体

    if let Some(hatch) = get_struct_escape_hatch(st) {
        generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
        generics_param_to_modify
                    .where_clause
                    .as_mut()
                    .unwrap()
                    .predicates
                    .push(syn::parse_str(hatch.as_str()).unwrap());
    } else {
        let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
        let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
        for field in fields {
            if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
                field_type_names.push(s);
            }
            if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
                type_param_names.push(s);
            }
        }

        let associated_types_map = get_generic_associated_types(st);
        for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
                let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
                if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                    continue;
                }
                if associated_types_map.contains_key(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                    continue;
                }
                t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//增加trait限定；(把Debug的这个tait增加到原有的语法树节点上)
            }
        }

        generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
        for (str, associated_types) in associated_types_map {
            eprintln!(">>>associated_types:{}", str);
            for associated_type in associated_types {
                generics_param_to_modify.where_clause.as_mut().unwrap()
                    .predicates.push(parse_quote!(#associated_type:std::fmt::Debug));
            }
        }
    }

    

    // 通过split_for_impl()函数把房型抽取出来形成三个impl的片段
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //\u62FC\u63A5\u6240\u6709\u6210\u5458\u53D8\u91CF
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//\u628ADebug\u7684\u8FD9\u4E2Atait\u589E\u52A0\u5230\u539F\u6709\u7684\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9\u4E0A
        }
    }

    // \u901A\u8FC7split_for_impl()\u51FD\u6570\u628A\u623F\u578B\u62BD\u53D6\u51FA\u6765\u5F62\u6210\u4E09\u4E2Aimpl\u7684\u7247\u6BB5
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

fn get_custom_format_of_field(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::NameValue(syn::MetaNameValue {
            ref path,
            ref lit,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if path.is_ident("debug") {
                if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = lit {
                    return Ok(Some(
                        ident_str.value()

                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)

fn generate_debug_fields(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token!(,)>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mut ret = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for field in fields.iter() {
        let field_name_ident = field.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let field_name_literal = field_name_ident.to_string();
        let mut format_str = "{:?}".to_string();
        if let Some(format) = get_custom_format_of_field(field)? {
            format_str = format;
        }
        
        ret.extend(quote::quote!( //\u62FC\u63A5\u6240\u6709\u6210\u5458\u53D8\u91CF
            .field(#field_name_literal, &format_args!(#format_str, &self.#field_name_ident))
        ));
    }
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_member_generic_type_name(field: &syn::Field, member_name: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ref arguments
        }) = segments.last() {
            if ident == member_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{
                    args,
                    ..
                }) = arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(syn::Type::Path(ref gp))) = args.first() {
                        if let Some(generic_ident) = gp.path.segments.first() {
                            return Ok(Some(generic_ident.ident.to_string()));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn get_field_type_name(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<String>> {
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{
        path: syn::Path{
            ref segments,
            ..
        },
        ..
    }) = field.ty {
        if let Some(syn::PathSegment{
            ref ident,
            ..
        }) = segments.last() {
            return Ok(Some(ident.to_string()));
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
    let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
    for field in fields {
        if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
            field_type_names.push(s);
        }
        if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
            type_param_names.push(s);
        }
    }

    eprintln!("field_type_name:{:?}", field_type_names);
    eprintln!("type_param_names:{:?}", type_param_names);

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
            if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                eprintln!(">>>>>>continue>>>>>>{} {:#?}", type_param_name, g);
                continue;
            }
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//\u589E\u52A0trait\u9650\u5B9A\uFF1B(\u628ADebug\u7684\u8FD9\u4E2Atait\u589E\u52A0\u5230\u539F\u6709\u7684\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9\u4E0A)
        }
    }

    // \u901A\u8FC7split_for_impl()\u51FD\u6570\u628A\u623F\u578B\u62BD\u53D6\u51FA\u6765\u5F62\u6210\u4E09\u4E2Aimpl\u7684\u7247\u6BB5
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

struct TypePathVisitor {
    generic_type_names: Vec<String>,
    associated_types: HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>>,
}

impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for TypePathVisitor {
    fn visit_type_path(&mut self, node:&'ast syn::TypePath) {
        eprintln!("node:{:#?}", node.path.segments);
        if node.path.segments.len() >= 2 {
            eprintln!("len > 2: {:#?}", node.path.segments);
            let generic_type_name = node.path.segments[0].ident.to_string();
            if self.generic_type_names.contains(&generic_type_name) {
                self.associated_types.entry(generic_type_name).or_insert(Vec::new()).push(node.clone());
            }
        }
        visit::visit_type_path(self, node);
    }
}
fn get_generic_associated_types(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>> {
    let origin_generic_param_names: Vec<String> = st.generics.params.iter().filter_map(
        |f| {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(ty) = f {
                return Some(ty.ident.to_string());
            }
            None
        }
    ).collect();

    let mut visitor = TypePathVisitor {
        generic_type_names: origin_generic_param_names,
        associated_types: HashMap::new(),
    };

    visitor.visit_derive_input(st);
    return visitor.associated_types;
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();

    let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
    let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
    for field in fields {
        if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
            field_type_names.push(s);
        }
        if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
            type_param_names.push(s);
        }
    }

    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;

    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();//\u83B7\u5F97DeriveInput\u4E2D\u7684\u6CDB\u578B\u53C2\u6570\u7ED3\u6784\u4F53
    let associated_types_map = get_generic_associated_types(st);
    for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
        if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
            let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
            if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                continue;
            }
            if associated_types_map.contains_key(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                continue;
            }
            t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//\u589E\u52A0trait\u9650\u5B9A\uFF1B(\u628ADebug\u7684\u8FD9\u4E2Atait\u589E\u52A0\u5230\u539F\u6709\u7684\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9\u4E0A)
        }
    }

    generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
    for (str, associated_types) in associated_types_map {
        eprintln!(">>>associated_types:{}", str);
        for associated_type in associated_types {
            generics_param_to_modify.where_clause.as_mut().unwrap()
                .predicates.push(parse_quote!(#associated_type:std::fmt::Debug));
        }
    }

    // \u901A\u8FC7split_for_impl()\u51FD\u6570\u628A\u623F\u578B\u62BD\u53D6\u51FA\u6765\u5F62\u6210\u4E09\u4E2Aimpl\u7684\u7247\u6BB5
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}
`})}),`
