struct TypePathVisitor {
    generic_type_names: Vec<String>,
    associated_types: HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>>,
}

impl<'ast> Visit<'ast> for TypePathVisitor {
    fn visit_type_path(&mut self, node:&'ast syn::TypePath) {
        eprintln!("node:{:#?}", node.path.segments);
        if node.path.segments.len() >= 2 {
            eprintln!("len > 2: {:#?}", node.path.segments);
            let generic_type_name = node.path.segments[0].ident.to_string();
            if self.generic_type_names.contains(&generic_type_name) {
                self.associated_types.entry(generic_type_name).or_insert(Vec::new()).push(node.clone());
            }
        }
        visit::visit_type_path(self, node);
    }
}
fn get_generic_associated_types(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> HashMap<String, Vec<syn::TypePath>> {
    let origin_generic_param_names: Vec<String> = st.generics.params.iter().filter_map(
        |f| {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(ty) = f {
                return Some(ty.ident.to_string());
            }
            None
        }
    ).collect();

    let mut visitor = TypePathVisitor {
        generic_type_names: origin_generic_param_names,
        associated_types: HashMap::new(),
    };

    visitor.visit_derive_input(st);
    return visitor.associated_types;
}

fn get_struct_escape_hatch(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> Option<String> {
    if let Some(insert_attr) = st.attrs.last() {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList{
            nested,
            ..
        })) = insert_attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Meta(syn::Meta::NameValue(path_value))) = nested.last() {
                if path_value.path.is_ident("bound") {
                    if let syn::Lit::Str(ref lit) = path_value.lit {
                        return Some(lit.value());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn do_expand(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let struct_name_ident = &st.ident;
    let struct_name_literal = struct_name_ident.to_string();
    let fields_stream = generate_debug_fields(fields)?;
    let mut generics_param_to_modify = st.generics.clone();//\u83B7\u5F97DeriveInput\u4E2D\u7684\u6CDB\u578B\u53C2\u6570\u7ED3\u6784\u4F53

    if let Some(hatch) = get_struct_escape_hatch(st) {
        generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
        generics_param_to_modify
                    .where_clause
                    .as_mut()
                    .unwrap()
                    .predicates
                    .push(syn::parse_str(hatch.as_str()).unwrap());
    } else {
        let mut field_type_names = Vec::new();
        let mut type_param_names = Vec::new();
        for field in fields {
            if let Some(s) = get_field_type_name(field)? {
                field_type_names.push(s);
            }
            if let Some(s) = get_member_generic_type_name(field, "PhantomData")? {
                type_param_names.push(s);
            }
        }

        let associated_types_map = get_generic_associated_types(st);
        for mut g in generics_param_to_modify.params.iter_mut() {
            if let syn::GenericParam::Type(t) = g {
                let type_param_name = t.ident.to_string();
                if type_param_names.contains(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                    continue;
                }
                if associated_types_map.contains_key(&type_param_name) && !field_type_names.contains(&type_param_name) {
                    continue;
                }
                t.bounds.push(parse_quote!(std::fmt::Debug));//\u589E\u52A0trait\u9650\u5B9A\uFF1B(\u628ADebug\u7684\u8FD9\u4E2Atait\u589E\u52A0\u5230\u539F\u6709\u7684\u8BED\u6CD5\u6811\u8282\u70B9\u4E0A)
            }
        }

        generics_param_to_modify.make_where_clause();
        for (str, associated_types) in associated_types_map {
            eprintln!(">>>associated_types:{}", str);
            for associated_type in associated_types {
                generics_param_to_modify.where_clause.as_mut().unwrap()
                    .predicates.push(parse_quote!(#associated_type:std::fmt::Debug));
            }
        }
    }

    

    // \u901A\u8FC7split_for_impl()\u51FD\u6570\u628A\u623F\u578B\u62BD\u53D6\u51FA\u6765\u5F62\u6210\u4E09\u4E2Aimpl\u7684\u7247\u6BB5
    let (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) = generics_param_to_modify.split_for_impl();


    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #impl_generics std::fmt::Debug for #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{
            fn fmt(&self, fmt: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
                fmt.debug_struct(#struct_name_literal)
                   #fields_stream
                   .finish() // We're good to go!
            }
        }
    );

    return Ok(ret);
}

`})})]})}function l(t={}){let{wrapper:e}=t.components||{};return e?(0,n.jsx)(e,{...t,children:(0,n.jsx)(o,{...t})}):o(t)}return k(T);})();
;return Component;1f:T4f7d,| 名字 | 版本号       |
| ---- | ------------ |
| rust | 1.69.0       |
| OS   | ubuntu 22.04 |

本题目不是proc-macro-workshop提供的builder/9道题中的其中任何一道题。本题是我在网上搜索派生宏的时候，无意中查询到的，没有看到派生宏代码，就粘贴下来当作是一道题，自己实现了。
题目代码是：
```rust
use derive_builder::BeanCheck;
use regex::{Regex, Captures};

#[derive(BeanCheck)]
struct UserDO {
    #[Min(30)]
    pub min_age: u32,
    #[Max(20)]
    #[Range(1, 100)]
    pub age: u16,
    #[Length(1, 13)]
    pub username: String,
    #[Pattern(r"^\d{1,5}$")]
    pub password: String,
    #[Email]
    pub email: String,
    pub mobile: String,
}

fn main() {
    let u = UserDO {
        min_age: 325,
        age: 20,
        username: "gorey".to_string(),
        password: "12345".to_string(),
        email: "aa@qq.com".to_string(),
        mobile: "13812341234".to_string()
    };

    match u.validate() {
        Ok(_) => { println!("check pass "); },
        Err(e) => { println!("{}", e); },
    }
}
```
这道题实际上就是为了一个struct结构体成员变量格式自动生成一个校验函数。通过惰性属性，分别为每一个成员变量生成对应不同的校验过程罢了。
直接上代码：
lib.rs
```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned};
use quote::{self, ToTokens};
use regex::{Regex, Captures};

type StructField = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructField> { //通过DeriveInput类型获得结构体内部的成员变量列表
    //这里使用了三种不同的实现方式
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct{
        fields:syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named, ..
        }), ..
    }) = st.data {
        return Ok(named)
    }
    //.........................................................
    // match st.data {
    //     syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct{
    //         fields:syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
    //             ref named, 
    //             ref brace_token
    //         }), ..
    //     }) => {
    //         return Ok(named);
    //     },
    //     _ => {},
    // }
    //.........................................................
    // match &st.data {
    //     syn::Data::Struct(ds) => {
    //         match &ds.fields {
    //             syn::Fields::Named(fieldName) => {
    //                 return Ok(&fieldName.named);
    //             },
    //             _ => {}
    //         }
    //     },
    //     _ => {},
    // }
    Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(st, "Must Define On Struct, Not On Enum".to_string()))
}
fn check_and_take_user_specified_from_ident(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<syn::Ident>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    match attr_str {
                        "Min" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                        r#"Min Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Max" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                        r#"Max Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Range" => {
                            let mut b = false;
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.last() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if b && nested.len() == 2{
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                        r#"Range Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Length" => {
                            let mut b = false;
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.last() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if b && nested.len() == 2 {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                        r#"Length Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Pattern" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Str(
                                syn::LitStr{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                        r#"Pattern Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        &_ => {},
                    }
                    //eprintln!("{} {:#?} {:#?}", attr_str, p.ident, nested.first());
                }
            } else {
                if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(ref o)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                        r#"xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                    ));
                }
                if let Ok(syn::Meta::Path(ref o)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                        o,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                        r#"xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::Path(syn::Path {
            ref segments,
            ref leading_colon,
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if segments.first().unwrap().ident == attr_str { //匹配Email
                // eprintln!(">path>{:#?}", segments);
                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                    segments.first().unwrap().ident.to_string().as_str(),
                    attr.span(),
                )));
            }
            // else {
            //     return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
            //         attr,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
            //         r#"unkown Error"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
            //     ));
            // }
        }
    }
    return Ok(None);
}

fn check_filter_fields<'a>(fields: &'a syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>, attr_str: &str) -> syn::Result<Vec<&'a syn::Field>> {
    let ret = fields.iter().filter(|item|{
        check_and_take_user_specified_from_ident(item, attr_str).unwrap().is_some()
    }).collect();
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_attribute_string(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<syn::LitStr> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Str(
                        litStr
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        /*
                        此处：
                            1. 无法创建新的LitStr对象，因为正则表达式前边的r字符无法被正确的转换
                            2. 无法返回引用，因为生命周期属于函数体
                            所以只能克隆一个对象然后返回
                        */
                        return Some(litStr.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_attribute_int(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<syn::LitInt> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        return Some(litInt.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_attribute_tuple(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<(syn::LitInt, syn::LitInt)> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    let mut left_value: syn::LitInt = syn::LitInt::new("0", attr.span());
                    let mut right_value: syn::LitInt = syn::LitInt::new("0", attr.span());
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        left_value = litInt.clone();    
                    };
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.last() {
                        right_value = litInt.clone();    
                    };
                    let ret = (left_value, right_value);
                    return Some(ret);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn generate_email_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let emails = check_filter_fields(fields, "Email")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = emails.iter().map(|f| {
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            let regex = Regex::new(r"^[A-Za-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]+@[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)+$").unwrap();
            if !regex.is_match(&self.#item_ident) {
                return Err(format!("The Email do not match: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , r"^[A-Za-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]+@[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)+$"));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_pattern_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let patterns = check_filter_fields(fields, "Pattern")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = patterns.iter().map(|f| {
        let pattern = get_attribute_string(f, "Pattern").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            let regex = Regex::new(#pattern).unwrap();
            if !regex.is_match(&self.#item_ident) {
                return Err(format!("The Email do not match: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #pattern));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_length_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let lengths = check_filter_fields(fields, "Length")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = lengths.iter().map(|f| {
        let (left_value, right_value) = get_attribute_tuple(f, "Length").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident.len() > #right_value && self.#item_ident.len() < #left_value {
                return Err(format!("The value exceeds the limit: {} : {} - {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #left_value, #right_value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_range_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let ranges = check_filter_fields(fields, "Range")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = ranges.iter().map(|f| {
        let (left_value, right_value) = get_attribute_tuple(f, "Range").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident > #right_value && self.#item_ident < #left_value {
                return Err(format!("The value exceeds the limit: {} : {} - {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #left_value, #right_value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_max_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let maxs = check_filter_fields(fields, "Max")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = maxs.iter().map(|f| {
        let value = get_attribute_int(f, "Max").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident > #value  {
                return Err(format!("The maximum value is greater than the set value: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_min_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mins = check_filter_fields(fields, "Min")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = mins.iter().map(|f| {
        let value = get_attribute_int(f, "Min").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident < #value  {
                return Err(format!("The minimum value is less than the set value: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret)    
}

fn do_expand_beancheck(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let struct_name = &st.ident;
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let email_func = generate_email_check(&fields)?;
    let pattern_func = generate_pattern_check(&fields)?;
    let length_func = generate_length_check(&fields)?;
    let range_func = generate_range_check(&fields)?;
    let max_func = generate_max_check(&fields)?;
    let min_func = generate_min_check(&fields)?;
    
    let str = "hello".to_string();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #struct_name {
            pub fn validate(&self) -> std::result::Result<&#struct_name, std::string::String> {
                #email_func
                #pattern_func
                #length_func
                #range_func
                #max_func
                #min_func
                Ok(self)
            }
        }
    );
    Ok(ret)
}

#[proc_macro_derive(BeanCheck, attributes(Min, Max, Range, Length, Pattern, Email))]
pub fn beancheck_derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand_beancheck(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

fn check_and_take_user_specified_from_ident(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> syn::Result<Option<syn::Ident>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    match attr_str {
                        "Min" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                        r#"Min Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Max" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                        r#"Max Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Range" => {
                            let mut b = false;
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.last() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if b && nested.len() == 2{
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                        r#"Range Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Length" => {
                            let mut b = false;
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                                syn::LitInt{..}
                            ))) = nested.last() {
                                b = true;
                            } else {
                                b = false;
                            }
                            if b && nested.len() == 2 {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {

                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                        r#"Length Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "Pattern" => {
                            if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Str(
                                syn::LitStr{..}
                            ))) = nested.first() {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    p.ident.to_string().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } else {
                                if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(ref o)) = nested.first() {
                                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                        r#"Pattern Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                    ));
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        &_ => {},
                    }
                    //eprintln!("{} {:#?} {:#?}", attr_str, p.ident, nested.first());
                }
            } else {
                if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(ref o)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                        r#"xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                    ));
                }
                if let Ok(syn::Meta::Path(ref o)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                    return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                        o,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                        r#"xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::Path(syn::Path {
            ref segments,
            ref leading_colon,
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if segments.first().unwrap().ident == attr_str { //\u5339\u914DEmail
                // eprintln!(">path>{:#?}", segments);
                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                    segments.first().unwrap().ident.to_string().as_str(),
                    attr.span(),
                )));
            }
            // else {
            //     return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
            //         attr,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
            //         r#"unkown Error"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
            //     ));
            // }
        }
    }
    return Ok(None);
}

fn check_filter_fields<'a>(fields: &'a syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>, attr_str: &str) -> syn::Result<Vec<&'a syn::Field>> {
    let ret = fields.iter().filter(|item|{
        check_and_take_user_specified_from_ident(item, attr_str).unwrap().is_some()
    }).collect();
    Ok(ret)
}

fn get_attribute_string(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<syn::LitStr> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Str(
                        litStr
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        /*
                        \u6B64\u5904\uFF1A
                            1. \u65E0\u6CD5\u521B\u5EFA\u65B0\u7684LitStr\u5BF9\u8C61\uFF0C\u56E0\u4E3A\u6B63\u5219\u8868\u8FBE\u5F0F\u524D\u8FB9\u7684r\u5B57\u7B26\u65E0\u6CD5\u88AB\u6B63\u786E\u7684\u8F6C\u6362
                            2. \u65E0\u6CD5\u8FD4\u56DE\u5F15\u7528\uFF0C\u56E0\u4E3A\u751F\u547D\u5468\u671F\u5C5E\u4E8E\u51FD\u6570\u4F53
                            \u6240\u4EE5\u53EA\u80FD\u514B\u9686\u4E00\u4E2A\u5BF9\u8C61\u7136\u540E\u8FD4\u56DE
                        */
                        return Some(litStr.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_attribute_int(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<syn::LitInt> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        return Some(litInt.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_attribute_tuple(field: &syn::Field, attr_str: &str) -> Option<(syn::LitInt, syn::LitInt)> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == attr_str {
                    let mut left_value: syn::LitInt = syn::LitInt::new("0", attr.span());
                    let mut right_value: syn::LitInt = syn::LitInt::new("0", attr.span());
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.first() {
                        left_value = litInt.clone();    
                    };
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Lit(syn::Lit::Int(
                        litInt
                    ))) = nested.last() {
                        right_value = litInt.clone();    
                    };
                    let ret = (left_value, right_value);
                    return Some(ret);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn generate_email_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let emails = check_filter_fields(fields, "Email")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = emails.iter().map(|f| {
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            let regex = Regex::new(r"^[A-Za-z0-9\\u4e00-\\u9fa5]+@[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)+$").unwrap();
            if !regex.is_match(&self.#item_ident) {
                return Err(format!("The Email do not match: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , r"^[A-Za-z0-9\\u4e00-\\u9fa5]+@[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)+$"));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_pattern_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let patterns = check_filter_fields(fields, "Pattern")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = patterns.iter().map(|f| {
        let pattern = get_attribute_string(f, "Pattern").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            let regex = Regex::new(#pattern).unwrap();
            if !regex.is_match(&self.#item_ident) {
                return Err(format!("The Email do not match: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #pattern));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_length_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let lengths = check_filter_fields(fields, "Length")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = lengths.iter().map(|f| {
        let (left_value, right_value) = get_attribute_tuple(f, "Length").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident.len() > #right_value && self.#item_ident.len() < #left_value {
                return Err(format!("The value exceeds the limit: {} : {} - {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #left_value, #right_value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_range_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let ranges = check_filter_fields(fields, "Range")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = ranges.iter().map(|f| {
        let (left_value, right_value) = get_attribute_tuple(f, "Range").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident > #right_value && self.#item_ident < #left_value {
                return Err(format!("The value exceeds the limit: {} : {} - {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #left_value, #right_value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_max_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let maxs = check_filter_fields(fields, "Max")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = maxs.iter().map(|f| {
        let value = get_attribute_int(f, "Max").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident > #value  {
                return Err(format!("The maximum value is greater than the set value: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret) 
}

fn generate_min_check(fields: &syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let mins = check_filter_fields(fields, "Min")?;
    let vec_ts: Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream> = mins.iter().map(|f| {
        let value = get_attribute_int(f, "Min").unwrap();
        let item_ident = f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        let _type = &f.ty;
        quote::quote!(
            if self.#item_ident < #value  {
                return Err(format!("The minimum value is less than the set value: {} : {}",stringify!(#item_ident) , #value));
            }
        )
    }).collect();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #(#vec_ts)*
    );
    Ok(ret)    
}

fn do_expand_beancheck(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {
    let struct_name = &st.ident;
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let email_func = generate_email_check(&fields)?;
    let pattern_func = generate_pattern_check(&fields)?;
    let length_func = generate_length_check(&fields)?;
    let range_func = generate_range_check(&fields)?;
    let max_func = generate_max_check(&fields)?;
    let min_func = generate_min_check(&fields)?;
    
    let str = "hello".to_string();
    let ret = quote::quote!(
        impl #struct_name {
            pub fn validate(&self) -> std::result::Result<&#struct_name, std::string::String> {
                #email_func
                #pattern_func
                #length_func
                #range_func
                #max_func
                #min_func
                Ok(self)
            }
        }
    );
    Ok(ret)
}

#[proc_macro_derive(BeanCheck, attributes(Min, Max, Range, Length, Pattern, Email))]
pub fn beancheck_derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream {
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expand_beancheck(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

builder这个目录的所有test主要测试的是派生宏的编写能力。
我就直接粘贴我的答案了。不做过多解释了。答案都是联通的，可以跑通builder 1-9关的测试用例。

```rust
use proc_macro::TokenStream;
use syn::{self, spanned::Spanned};
use quote::{self, ToTokens};
use regex::{Regex, Captures};

#[proc_macro_derive(Builder, attributes(builder))]
pub fn derive(input: TokenStream) -> TokenStream { //派生宏主函数
    let st = syn::parse_macro_input!(input as syn::DeriveInput);
    match do_expend(&st) {
        Ok(token_stream) => token_stream.into(),
        Err(e) => e.to_compile_error().into(),
    }
}

type StructField = syn::punctuated::Punctuated<syn::Field, syn::Token![,]>;
fn get_fields_from_derive_input(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<&StructField> { //通过DeriveInput类型获得结构体内部的成员变量列表
    //这里使用了三种不同的实现方式
    if let syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct{
        fields:syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
            ref named, ..
        }), ..
    }) = st.data {
        return Ok(named)
    }
    //.........................................................
    // match st.data {
    //     syn::Data::Struct(syn::DataStruct{
    //         fields:syn::Fields::Named(syn::FieldsNamed{
    //             ref named, 
    //             ref brace_token
    //         }), ..
    //     }) => {
    //         return Ok(named);
    //     },
    //     _ => {},
    // }
    //.........................................................
    // match &st.data {
    //     syn::Data::Struct(ds) => {
    //         match &ds.fields {
    //             syn::Fields::Named(fieldName) => {
    //                 return Ok(&fieldName.named);
    //             },
    //             _ => {}
    //         }
    //     },
    //     _ => {},
    // }
    Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(st, "Must Define On Struct, Not On Enum".to_string()))
}

fn generate_builder_struct_fields_def(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//构建结构体CommandBuilder内部成员变量的循环生成
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let idents: Vec<_> = fields.iter().map(|f| &f.ident).collect();
    let types: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        if let Some(inner_type) = get_generic_inner_type(&f.ty, "Option") {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                std::option::Option<#inner_type>
            ))
        } else if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(f)?.is_some() {
            let origin_type = &f.ty;
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #origin_type
            ))
        } else {
            let origin_type = &f.ty;
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                std::option::Option<#origin_type>
            ))
        }
    }).collect();

    let types = types?;

    let ret = quote::quote!(
        #( #idents: #types ),*
    );
    Ok(ret)
}

fn generate_builder_struct_factory_init_clauses(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<Vec<proc_macro2::TokenStream>> {//Command的builder函数获得CommandBuilder对象，本函数实现CommandBuilder对象内的属性循环生成
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let init_cluase: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        let ident = &f.ident;
        if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&f)?.is_some() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: std::vec::Vec::new()
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: std::option::Option::None
            ))
        }
    }).collect();
    Ok(init_cluase?)
}

fn generate_setter_functions(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//生成一堆setter函数
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    let idents: Vec<_> = fields.iter().map(|f| &f.ident).collect();
    let types: Vec<_> = fields.iter().map(|f| &f.ty).collect();

    let mut final_tokenstream = proc_macro2::TokenStream::new();
    for (idx, (ident,type_)) in idents.iter().zip(types.iter()).enumerate() {//zip把两个不同的iter迭代器，压缩到一起形成一个元祖类型，然后我们用模式匹配获取元祖中的值
        let tokenstream_piece = if let Some(inner_type) = get_generic_inner_type(type_, "Option") {
            quote::quote!(
                fn #ident(&mut self, #ident: #inner_type) -> &mut Self {
                    self.#ident = std::option::Option::Some(#ident);
                    self
                }
            )
        } else if let Some(ref user_specified_ident) = get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&fields[idx])? {
            let inner_type 
                = get_generic_inner_type(type_, "Vec")
                    .ok_or(syn::Error::new(fields[idx].span(), "`each` field must be a Vec Type"))?;
            
            let mut r = quote::quote!(
                fn #user_specified_ident(&mut self, #user_specified_ident: #inner_type) -> &mut Self {
                    self.#ident.push(#user_specified_ident);
                    self
                }
            );
            if user_specified_ident != ident.as_ref().unwrap() {
                r.extend(
                    quote::quote!(
                        fn #ident(&mut self, #ident:#type_) -> &mut Self {
                            self.#ident = #ident.clone();
                            self
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
            r
        } else {
            quote::quote!(
                fn #ident(&mut self, #ident: #type_) -> &mut Self {
                    self.#ident = std::option::Option::Some(#ident);
                    self
                }
            )
        };
        final_tokenstream.extend(tokenstream_piece);//追加tokenstream到最终tokenstream
    }
    Ok(final_tokenstream)
}

fn generate_build_function(st: &syn::DeriveInput, struct_name_literal: &str) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//给CommandBuilder构建builder函数，用来实现Command
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    //第一种实现
    // let struct_name_literal = syn::Ident::new(&struct_name_literal, st.span());
    // let mut checker_code_pieces = Vec::new();
    // for idx in 0..fields.len() {
    //     let ident = &fields[idx].ident;
    //     if get_generic_inner_type(&fields[idx].ty, "Option").is_none() && 
    //         get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&fields[idx])?.is_none(){
    //         checker_code_pieces.push( //利用stringfy!宏，把ident类型转换成string
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 if self.#ident.is_none() {
    //                     let err = format!("{} field is missing", stringify!(#ident)); 
    //                     return std::result::Result::Err(err.into());
    //                 }
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else {
    //         checker_code_pieces.push( 
    //             proc_macro2::TokenStream::new()
    //         );
    //     }
    // }

    // let mut fill_result_class = Vec::new();
    // for idx in 0..fields.len() {
    //     let ident = &fields[idx].ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        
    //     if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&fields[idx])?.is_some() {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else if get_generic_inner_type(&fields[idx].ty, "Option").is_none() {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone().unwrap()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     }
    // }
    // let token_stream = quote::quote!(
    //     pub fn build(&mut self) -> 
    //         std::result::Result<#struct_name_literal, std::boxed::Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    //         #( #checker_code_pieces )*

    //         let ret = #struct_name_literal {
    //             #( #fill_result_class ),*
    //         };
    //         return std::result::Result::Ok(ret);
    //     }
    // );
    // Ok(token_stream)

    //第二种实现
    let loop_field: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        let ident = &f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        if get_generic_inner_type(&f.ty, "Option").is_none() && 
            get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&f)?.is_none() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                if self.#ident.is_none() {
                    let err = format!("{} field is missing", stringify!(#ident)); 
                    return std::result::Result::Err(err.into());
                }
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(proc_macro2::TokenStream::new())
        }
    }).collect();
    let loop_field = loop_field?;

    let struct_field: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        let ident = &f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&f)?.is_some() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone()
            ))
        } else if get_generic_inner_type(&f.ty, "Option").is_none() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone().unwrap()
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone()
            ))
        }
    }).collect();
    let struct_field = struct_field?;
    let struct_name_literal = syn::Ident::new(struct_name_literal, st.span());

    let ret = quote::quote!(
        pub fn build(&mut self) -> std::result::Result<#struct_name_literal, std::boxed::Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
            #( #loop_field )*

            let ret = #struct_name_literal {
                #( #struct_field ),*
            };
            return std::result::Result::Ok(ret);
        }
    );
    return Ok(ret);

}

fn get_generic_inner_type<'a>(t: &'a syn::Type, outer_ident_name: &str) -> Option<&'a syn::Type> {// 匹配获得成员变量属性
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{ 
        path: syn::Path{segments ,..}, ..}) = t {
        if let Some(seg) = segments.last() {
            if seg.ident.to_string() == outer_ident_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(
                    syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{args,..} 
                ) = &seg.arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(inner_type)) = args.first() {
                        return Some(inner_type);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<syn::Ident>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == "builder" {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Meta(syn::Meta::NameValue(kv))) = nested.first() {
                        if kv.path.is_ident("each") {
                            if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = kv.lit {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    ident_str.value().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } 
                        } else {
                            if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(ref list)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                                return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                    list,                             //第一个参数，决定了报错的范围，‘^'符号指向的范围
                                    r#"expected `builder(each = "...")`"#                   //第二个参数，决定了报错的错误信息内容
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn do_expend(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//展开拼接TokenStream的总函数
    let struct_name_literal =  st.ident.to_string();
    let builder_name_literal = format!("{}Builder", struct_name_literal);
    let builder_name_ident = syn::Ident::new(&builder_name_literal, st.span());
    let struct_ident = &st.ident;
    let builder_struct_field_ref = generate_builder_struct_fields_def(st)?;
    let builder_struct_factory_init_clauses = generate_builder_struct_factory_init_clauses(st)?;
    let setter_functions = generate_setter_functions(st)?;
    let build_function = generate_build_function(st, &struct_name_literal)?;

    let ret = quote::quote! {
        pub struct #builder_name_ident {
            #builder_struct_field_ref //展开TokenStream
        }
        impl #struct_ident {
            pub fn builder() -> #builder_name_ident {
                #builder_name_ident {
                    #( #builder_struct_factory_init_clauses ),* //展开Vec<TokenStream>
                }
            }
        }
        impl #builder_name_ident {
            #setter_functions
        }

        impl #builder_name_ident {
            #build_function
        }
    };
    Ok(ret)
}

                    self.#ident = std::option::Option::Some(#ident);

                }

                    .ok_or(syn::Error::new(fields[idx].span(), "\`each\` field must be a Vec Type"))?;
            
            let mut r = quote::quote!(
                fn #user_specified_ident(&mut self, #user_specified_ident: #inner_type) -> &mut Self {
                    self.#ident.push(#user_specified_ident);
                    self
                }
            );
            if user_specified_ident != ident.as_ref().unwrap() {
                r.extend(
                    quote::quote!(
                        fn #ident(&mut self, #ident:#type_) -> &mut Self {
                            self.#ident = #ident.clone();
                            self
                        }
                    )
                );
            }
            r
        } else {
            quote::quote!(
                fn #ident(&mut self, #ident: #type_) -> &mut Self {
                    self.#ident = std::option::Option::Some(#ident);
                    self
                }
            )
        };
        final_tokenstream.extend(tokenstream_piece);//\u8FFD\u52A0tokenstream\u5230\u6700\u7EC8tokenstream
    }
    Ok(final_tokenstream)
}

fn generate_build_function(st: &syn::DeriveInput, struct_name_literal: &str) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//\u7ED9CommandBuilder\u6784\u5EFAbuilder\u51FD\u6570\uFF0C\u7528\u6765\u5B9E\u73B0Command
    let fields = get_fields_from_derive_input(st)?;
    //\u7B2C\u4E00\u79CD\u5B9E\u73B0
    // let struct_name_literal = syn::Ident::new(&struct_name_literal, st.span());
    // let mut checker_code_pieces = Vec::new();
    // for idx in 0..fields.len() {
    //     let ident = &fields[idx].ident;
    //     if get_generic_inner_type(&fields[idx].ty, "Option").is_none() && 
    //         get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&fields[idx])?.is_none(){
    //         checker_code_pieces.push( //\u5229\u7528stringfy!\u5B8F\uFF0C\u628Aident\u7C7B\u578B\u8F6C\u6362\u6210string
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 if self.#ident.is_none() {
    //                     let err = format!("{} field is missing", stringify!(#ident)); 
    //                     return std::result::Result::Err(err.into());
    //                 }
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else {
    //         checker_code_pieces.push( 
    //             proc_macro2::TokenStream::new()
    //         );
    //     }
    // }

    // let mut fill_result_class = Vec::new();
    // for idx in 0..fields.len() {
    //     let ident = &fields[idx].ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        
    //     if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&fields[idx])?.is_some() {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else if get_generic_inner_type(&fields[idx].ty, "Option").is_none() {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone().unwrap()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     } else {
    //         fill_result_class.push(
    //             quote::quote!(
    //                 #ident: self.#ident.clone()
    //             )
    //         );
    //     }
    // }
    // let token_stream = quote::quote!(
    //     pub fn build(&mut self) -> 
    //         std::result::Result<#struct_name_literal, std::boxed::Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    //         #( #checker_code_pieces )*

    //         let ret = #struct_name_literal {
    //             #( #fill_result_class ),*
    //         };
    //         return std::result::Result::Ok(ret);
    //     }
    // );
    // Ok(token_stream)

    //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u79CD\u5B9E\u73B0
    let loop_field: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        let ident = &f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        if get_generic_inner_type(&f.ty, "Option").is_none() && 
            get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&f)?.is_none() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                if self.#ident.is_none() {
                    let err = format!("{} field is missing", stringify!(#ident)); 
                    return std::result::Result::Err(err.into());
                }
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(proc_macro2::TokenStream::new())
        }
    }).collect();
    let loop_field = loop_field?;

    let struct_field: syn::Result<Vec<_>> = fields.iter().map(|f| {
        let ident = &f.ident.as_ref().unwrap();
        if get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(&f)?.is_some() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone()
            ))
        } else if get_generic_inner_type(&f.ty, "Option").is_none() {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone().unwrap()
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(quote::quote!(
                #ident: self.#ident.clone()
            ))
        }
    }).collect();
    let struct_field = struct_field?;
    let struct_name_literal = syn::Ident::new(struct_name_literal, st.span());

    let ret = quote::quote!(
        pub fn build(&mut self) -> std::result::Result<#struct_name_literal, std::boxed::Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
            #( #loop_field )*

            let ret = #struct_name_literal {
                #( #struct_field ),*
            };
            return std::result::Result::Ok(ret);
        }
    );
    return Ok(ret);

}

fn get_generic_inner_type<'a>(t: &'a syn::Type, outer_ident_name: &str) -> Option<&'a syn::Type> {// \u5339\u914D\u83B7\u5F97\u6210\u5458\u53D8\u91CF\u5C5E\u6027
    if let syn::Type::Path(syn::TypePath{ 
        path: syn::Path{segments ,..}, ..}) = t {
        if let Some(seg) = segments.last() {
            if seg.ident.to_string() == outer_ident_name {
                if let syn::PathArguments::AngleBracketed(
                    syn::AngleBracketedGenericArguments{args,..} 
                ) = &seg.arguments {
                    if let Some(syn::GenericArgument::Type(inner_type)) = args.first() {
                        return Some(inner_type);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn get_user_specified_ident_for_vec(field: &syn::Field) -> syn::Result<Option<syn::Ident>> {
    for attr in &field.attrs {
        if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(syn::MetaList {
            ref path,
            ref nested,
            ..
        })) = attr.parse_meta() {
            if let Some(p) = path.segments.first() {
                if p.ident == "builder" {
                    if let Some(syn::NestedMeta::Meta(syn::Meta::NameValue(kv))) = nested.first() {
                        if kv.path.is_ident("each") {
                            if let syn::Lit::Str(ref ident_str) = kv.lit {
                                return Ok(Some(syn::Ident::new(
                                    ident_str.value().as_str(),
                                    attr.span(),
                                )));
                            } 
                        } else {
                            if let Ok(syn::Meta::List(ref list)) = attr.parse_meta() {
                                return Err(syn::Error::new_spanned( 
                                    list,                             //\u7B2C\u4E00\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u8303\u56F4\uFF0C\u2018^'\u7B26\u53F7\u6307\u5411\u7684\u8303\u56F4
                                    r#"expected \`builder(each = "...")\`"#                   //\u7B2C\u4E8C\u4E2A\u53C2\u6570\uFF0C\u51B3\u5B9A\u4E86\u62A5\u9519\u7684\u9519\u8BEF\u4FE1\u606F\u5185\u5BB9
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

fn do_expend(st: &syn::DeriveInput) -> syn::Result<proc_macro2::TokenStream> {//\u5C55\u5F00\u62FC\u63A5TokenStream\u7684\u603B\u51FD\u6570
    let struct_name_literal =  st.ident.to_string();
    let builder_name_literal = format!("{}Builder", struct_name_literal);
    let builder_name_ident = syn::Ident::new(&builder_name_literal, st.span());
    let struct_ident = &st.ident;
    let builder_struct_field_ref = generate_builder_struct_fields_def(st)?;
    let builder_struct_factory_init_clauses = generate_builder_struct_factory_init_clauses(st)?;
    let setter_functions = generate_setter_functions(st)?;
    let build_function = generate_build_function(st, &struct_name_literal)?;

    let ret = quote::quote! {
        pub struct #builder_name_ident {
            #builder_struct_field_ref //\u5C55\u5F00TokenStream
        }
        impl #struct_ident {
            pub fn builder() -> #builder_name_ident {
                #builder_name_ident {
                    #( #builder_struct_factory_init_clauses ),* //\u5C55\u5F00Vec<TokenStream>
                }
            }
        }
        impl #builder_name_ident {
            #setter_functions
        }

        impl #builder_name_ident {
            #build_function
        }
    };
    Ok(ret)
}

proc-macro-workshop是一个学习rust过程宏非常好的工程。里边包含五大类题目。并且每种题目都有实际价值，可以应用在企业级项目中。我们在这里先介绍一下这个项目如何运行如何测试，如何验证过程宏的正确性以及如何调试。
本文只围绕以下几个话题：
第一：工程结构？
第二：如何运行测试？
第三：如何调试自己开发的过程宏？
首先到网站[https://github.com/dtolnay/proc-macro-workshop](https://github.com/dtolnay/proc-macro-workshop)下载这个工程。
![请添加图片描述](../images/31432a0e2e48dbc010a82efaf964e0c6.png)
这里面的目录bitfield,builder,debug,seq,sorted。就是这个工程的五个题目了。
我们先进入到第一题的builder目录看一下。
![请添加图片描述](../images/5b7abe7e928a13a018e9da1129915b85.png)
大家可以看到这个工程的目录结构了。当然了原题目只有9个。我自己加了一个，还在网上找了一个，并且全都实现了，这个话题我们后边说，我们这里只介绍这个项目如何运行，如何测试。

- 每一个题目的目录中都需要在当前题目目录/src/lib.rs这个文件中加入自己的答案。
- 在tests目录中都有一个progress.rs文件，打开里边注释的对应题目的行就可以进行测试了。
![请添加图片描述](../images/20bc80bb6ff640d68ed3b8273222b01e.png)
# 如何运行这个progress.rs测试呢？
第一：在题目目录的Cargo.toml加入依赖，一开始上来，每一个题目目录中的Cargo.toml中的依赖都是空的，都需要我们自己加入。
第二：在命令方进入工程目录，然后进入题目目录。然后在这个目录直接执行cargo test命令，就可以运行这个progress.rs文件进行标准测试了。
```rust
rust_workspace/rockjl_proc-macro-workshop/builder$ 
rust_workspace/rockjl_proc-macro-workshop/builder$ cargo test
```
<font color="red">我们直接写的过程宏，只要能通过测试就算是过关了</font>
# 如何调试自己的过程宏？
第一：过程宏属于编译期运行的。所以debug肯定不好使。所以第一种调试方式就是命令方大法。但是不可是使用标准输出，会被拦截。必须使用错误输出eprintln!这个宏，才可以。使用这个宏，加入到lib.rs文件中，打印自己开发的过程宏代码中，就可以进行测试了。

第二：把题目粘贴到最外层的main.rs中，然后在vscode中使用F5，运行一下整个工程。如果没有任何报错，就证明当前过程宏编译没有错误。

第三：安装nightly，可以看到过程宏展开以后的代码。这样的话，我们可以进行精准调试。
首先显示第二种把题目粘贴到main.rs。然后安装nightly(这个可以自行百度)版本。最后进入工程主目录，执行cargo expand命令，就可以查看展开以后的代码了。

```rust
rust_workspace/rockjl_proc-macro-workshop$ 
rust_workspace/rockjl_proc-macro-workshop$ cargo expand
```
![请添加图片描述](../images/474aa7b3090450026b7379a99b463658.png)
最后这些展开的代码同样可以粘贴到main.rs中，查看代码的正确性。24:T1e59,var Component=(()=>{var p=Object.create;var o=Object.defineProperty;var g=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor;var m=Object.getOwnPropertyNames;var u=Object.getPrototypeOf,b=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty;var k=(e,r)=>()=>(r||e((r={exports:{}}).exports,r),r.exports),_=(e,r)=>{for(var c in r)o(e,c,{get:r[c],enumerable:!0})},a=(e,r,c,l)=>{if(r&&typeof r=="object"||typeof r=="function")for(let t of m(r))!b.call(e,t)&&t!==c&&o(e,t,{get:()=>r[t],enumerable:!(l=g(r,t))||l.enumerable});return e};var w=(e,r,c)=>(c=e!=null?p(u(e)):{},a(r||!e||!e.__esModule?o(c,"default",{value:e,enumerable:!0}):c,e)),f=e=>a(o({},"__esModule",{value:!0}),e);var d=k((y,s)=>{s.exports=_jsx_runtime});var x={};_(x,{default:()=>h});var n=w(d());function i(e){let r={a:"a",code:"code",h1:"h1",img:"img",li:"li",p:"p",pre:"pre",table:"table",tbody:"tbody",td:"td",th:"th",thead:"thead",tr:"tr",ul:"ul",...e.components};return(0,n.jsxs)(n.Fragment,{children:[(0,n.jsxs)(r.table,{children:[(0,n.jsx)(r.thead,{children:(0,n.jsxs)(r.tr,{children:[(0,n.jsx)(r.th,{children:"\u540D\u5B57"}),(0,n.jsx)(r.th,{children:"\u7248\u672C\u53F7"})]})}),(0,n.jsxs)(r.tbody,{children:[(0,n.jsxs)(r.tr,{children:[(0,n.jsx)(r.td,{children:"rust"}),(0,n.jsx)(r.td,{children:"1.69.0"})]}),(0,n.jsxs)(r.tr,{children:[(0,n.jsx)(r.td,{children:"OS"}),(0,n.jsx)(r.td,{children:"ubuntu22.04"})]})]})]}),`

## 先看结构
test_lib目录结构
![在这里插入图片描述](../images/ed07084d65be3a396b8180b3d18ed6ac.png)
这与另一个crate处于同一个目录，所以另一crate倒入的时候在Cargo.toml中使用如下语句。

```bash
test_lib = { path = "../test_lib" }
```
先在test_lib/src/abc/abc.rs中添加没有cfg的两个函数做测试。
```rust
pub fn abc() {
    println!("abc");
}

pub fn def() {
    println!("def");
}
```
然后在使用的crate的main.rs中加入如下语句，这个时候发现是可以完美执行的。
```rust
test_lib::abc::abc::abc();
test_lib::abc::abc::def();
```
## cfg
现在正式开始来说cfg。这个cfg分为两个方面。
feature与直接写。
### 先看feature。
1. 在Cargo.toml中添加自己的feature
```bash
	[features]
	rockjiang = []
```
2. 在函数前面增加cfg
```rust
	#[cfg(feature = "rockjiang")]
	pub fn def() {
	    println!("def");
	}
```
3. 在使用的crate的Cargo.toml中需要增加如下写法，否则函数def将无法被找到。
```bash
	test_lib = { path = "../test_lib", features = ["rockjiang"]}
```
### 再来看直接在cfg中增加内容的
在函数abc前面增加cfg
```rust
	#[cfg(rockjiang_unstable)]
	pub fn abc() {
	    println!("abc");
	}
```
这种情况有两种解决方案，一个是增加环境变量，另一个是使用config.toml文件。
#### 编译前增加环境变量
1. 编译前增加环境变量
```bash
	export RUSTFLAGS="--cfg rockjiang_unstable"
```
	这里如果有多个，可以使用空格分割，继续增加多个
```bash
	export RUSTFLAGS="--cfg rockjiang_unstable --cfg xxx11 --cfg xxxxx2"
```
2. 编译运行，还是最简单的原始编译命令。但是如果没有提前增加前面步骤的环境变量。则上面abc函数将无法被找到。
```bash
	cargo build
```
#### 使用config.toml文件
1. 编译前创建config.toml文件并添加如下内容
```bash
	[build]
	rustflags = ["--cfg", "tokio_unstable"]
```
	目录结构是
	![在这里插入图片描述](../images/8e645a134bfe6d65c65389c0ce0af245.png)

2. 编译运行cargo build
## 总结
当然了还要有综合用法。
```rust
#[cfg(rockjiang_unstable, feature = "rockjiang")]
pub fn abc() {
    println!("abc");
}
```

## 查看当前系统支持的交叉编译指定系统版本列表
```bash
rustup target list
```
如果已经安装这里会显示（installed）。
![在这里插入图片描述](../images/edd669766a79c99a0f4b833cfd0923b9.png)
## 安装target与对应的链接器
linux通常就是musl和gnu。我用的测试系统是ubuntu，所以是gnu。
### musl
```bash
rustup target add x86_64-unknown-linux-musl
```
```bash
brew install filosottile/musl-cross/musl-cross
```
### gnu
```bash
rustup target add x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
```
```bash
brew install SergioBenitez/osxct/x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
```
### windows
```bash
rustup target add x86_64-pc-windows-gnu
```
```bash
brew install mingw-w64
```
## 配置工程
如下配置添加到Cargo.toml
```xml
[target.x86_64-unknown-linux-musl] # 也支持[build]节点 设置target=...
linker = "x86_64-linux-musl-gcc"
[target.x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu]
linker = "x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-gcc"
[target.x86_64-pc-windows-gnu]
linker = "x86_64-w64-mingw32-gcc"
```

## MAC下编译
### ubuntu
```bash
cargo build --release --target=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
//如果这条命令编译出问题，可以尝试下面的命令
cargo zigbuild --release --target=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
```
### windows
```bash
cargo build --release --target=x86_64-pc-windows-gnu`
```
## 问题补充
### 如果碰到openssl问题
那一定是openssl与rust的编译环境没有配置好。
使用如下语句配置编译环境，即可通过。
```bash
OPENSSL_LIB_DIR=/usr/lib OPENSSL_INCLUDE_DIR=/usr/include/openssl PKG_CONFIG_PATH=/usr/lib/pkgconfig CARGO_TARGET_DIR="./target" cargo build --release --target x86_64-pc-windows-gnu
```
### 实在不行可以尝试一下zigbuild
